Skip for time-pressured decisions, mechanical tasks, lookups, or when the user wants a recommendation rather than a tour of considerations. For a decision with genuine multi-stakeholder stakes spanning several teams, or organization-scale, hard-to-reverse consequences, use reasoning-swarm instead — its independent-parallel-agent dispatch is worth the extra cost there. ([ravenoak/llm-skills](https://github.com/ravenoak/llm-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/reasoning-framework) · 1 skill)
